     1→// Package settings provides configuration loading for Entire.
     2→// This package is separate from cli to allow strategy package to import it
     3→// without creating an import cycle (cli imports strategy).
     4→package settings
     5→
     6→import (
     7→	"bytes"
     8→	"encoding/json"
     9→	"fmt"
    10→	"os"
    11→	"path/filepath"
    12→
    13→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/jsonutil"
    14→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    15→)
    16→
    17→// DefaultStrategyName is the default strategy when none is configured.
    18→// This is duplicated here to avoid importing the strategy package (which would create a cycle).
    19→const DefaultStrategyName = "manual-commit"
    20→
    21→const (
    22→	// EntireSettingsFile is the path to the Entire settings file
    23→	EntireSettingsFile = ".entire/settings.json"
    24→	// EntireSettingsLocalFile is the path to the local settings override file (not committed)
    25→	EntireSettingsLocalFile = ".entire/settings.local.json"
    26→)
    27→
    28→// EntireSettings represents the .entire/settings.json configuration
    29→type EntireSettings struct {
    30→	// Strategy is the name of the git strategy to use
    31→	Strategy string `json:"strategy"`
    32→
    33→	// Enabled indicates whether Entire is active. When false, CLI commands
    34→	// show a disabled message and hooks exit silently. Defaults to true.
    35→	Enabled bool `json:"enabled"`
    36→
    37→	// LocalDev indicates whether to use "go run" instead of the "entire" binary
    38→	// This is used for development when the binary is not installed
    39→	LocalDev bool `json:"local_dev,omitempty"`
    40→
    41→	// LogLevel sets the logging verbosity (debug, info, warn, error).
    42→	// Can be overridden by ENTIRE_LOG_LEVEL environment variable.
    43→	// Defaults to "info".
    44→	LogLevel string `json:"log_level,omitempty"`
    45→
    46→	// StrategyOptions contains strategy-specific configuration
    47→	StrategyOptions map[string]any `json:"strategy_options,omitempty"`
    48→
    49→	// Telemetry controls anonymous usage analytics.
    50→	// nil = not asked yet (show prompt), true = opted in, false = opted out
    51→	Telemetry *bool `json:"telemetry,omitempty"`
    52→}
    53→
    54→// Load loads the Entire settings from .entire/settings.json,
    55→// then applies any overrides from .entire/settings.local.json if it exists.
    56→// Returns default settings if neither file exists.
    57→// Works correctly from any subdirectory within the repository.
    58→func Load() (*EntireSettings, error) {
    59→	// Get absolute paths for settings files
    60→	settingsFileAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(EntireSettingsFile)
    61→	if err != nil {
    62→		settingsFileAbs = EntireSettingsFile // Fallback to relative
    63→	}
    64→	localSettingsFileAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(EntireSettingsLocalFile)
    65→	if err != nil {
    66→		localSettingsFileAbs = EntireSettingsLocalFile // Fallback to relative
    67→	}
    68→
    69→	// Load base settings
    70→	settings, err := loadFromFile(settingsFileAbs)
    71→	if err != nil {
    72→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("reading settings file: %w", err)
    73→	}
    74→
    75→	// Apply local overrides if they exist
    76→	localData, err := os.ReadFile(localSettingsFileAbs) //nolint:gosec // path is from AbsPath or constant
    77→	if err != nil {
    78→		if !os.IsNotExist(err) {
    79→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("reading local settings file: %w", err)
    80→		}
    81→		// Local file doesn't exist, continue without overrides
    82→	} else {
    83→		if err := mergeJSON(settings, localData); err != nil {
    84→			return nil, fmt.Errorf("merging local settings: %w", err)
    85→		}
    86→	}
    87→
    88→	applyDefaults(settings)
    89→
    90→	return settings, nil
    91→}
    92→
    93→// LoadFromFile loads settings from a specific file path without merging local overrides.
    94→// Returns default settings if the file doesn't exist.
    95→// Use this when you need to display individual settings files separately.
    96→func LoadFromFile(filePath string) (*EntireSettings, error) {
    97→	return loadFromFile(filePath)
    98→}
    99→
   100→// loadFromFile loads settings from a specific file path.
   101→// Returns default settings if the file doesn't exist.
   102→func loadFromFile(filePath string) (*EntireSettings, error) {
   103→	settings := &EntireSettings{
   104→		Strategy: DefaultStrategyName,
   105→		Enabled:  true, // Default to enabled
   106→	}
   107→
   108→	data, err := os.ReadFile(filePath) //nolint:gosec // path is from caller
   109→	if err != nil {
   110→		if os.IsNotExist(err) {
   111→			return settings, nil
   112→		}
   113→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w", err)
   114→	}
   115→
   116→	dec := json.NewDecoder(bytes.NewReader(data))
   117→	dec.DisallowUnknownFields()
   118→	if err := dec.Decode(settings); err != nil {
   119→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("parsing settings file: %w", err)
   120→	}
   121→	applyDefaults(settings)
   122→
   123→	return settings, nil
   124→}
   125→
   126→// mergeJSON merges JSON data into existing settings.
   127→// Only non-zero values from the JSON override existing settings.
   128→func mergeJSON(settings *EntireSettings, data []byte) error {
   129→	// First, validate that there are no unknown keys using strict decoding
   130→	dec := json.NewDecoder(bytes.NewReader(data))
   131→	dec.DisallowUnknownFields()
   132→	var temp EntireSettings
   133→	if err := dec.Decode(&temp); err != nil {
   134→		return fmt.Errorf("parsing JSON: %w", err)
   135→	}
   136→
   137→	// Parse into a map to check which fields are present
   138→	var raw map[string]json.RawMessage
   139→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &raw); err != nil {
   140→		return fmt.Errorf("parsing JSON: %w", err)
   141→	}
   142→
   143→	// Override strategy if present and non-empty
   144→	if strategyRaw, ok := raw["strategy"]; ok {
   145→		var s string
   146→		if err := json.Unmarshal(strategyRaw, &s); err != nil {
   147→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing strategy field: %w", err)
   148→		}
   149→		if s != "" {
   150→			settings.Strategy = s
   151→		}
   152→	}
   153→
   154→	// Override enabled if present
   155→	if enabledRaw, ok := raw["enabled"]; ok {
   156→		var e bool
   157→		if err := json.Unmarshal(enabledRaw, &e); err != nil {
   158→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing enabled field: %w", err)
   159→		}
   160→		settings.Enabled = e
   161→	}
   162→
   163→	// Override local_dev if present
   164→	if localDevRaw, ok := raw["local_dev"]; ok {
   165→		var ld bool
   166→		if err := json.Unmarshal(localDevRaw, &ld); err != nil {
   167→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing local_dev field: %w", err)
   168→		}
   169→		settings.LocalDev = ld
   170→	}
   171→
   172→	// Override log_level if present and non-empty
   173→	if logLevelRaw, ok := raw["log_level"]; ok {
   174→		var ll string
   175→		if err := json.Unmarshal(logLevelRaw, &ll); err != nil {
   176→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing log_level field: %w", err)
   177→		}
   178→		if ll != "" {
   179→			settings.LogLevel = ll
   180→		}
   181→	}
   182→
   183→	// Merge strategy_options if present
   184→	if optionsRaw, ok := raw["strategy_options"]; ok {
   185→		var opts map[string]any
   186→		if err := json.Unmarshal(optionsRaw, &opts); err != nil {
   187→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing strategy_options field: %w", err)
   188→		}
   189→		if settings.StrategyOptions == nil {
   190→			settings.StrategyOptions = opts
   191→		} else {
   192→			for k, v := range opts {
   193→				settings.StrategyOptions[k] = v
   194→			}
   195→		}
   196→	}
   197→
   198→	// Override telemetry if present
   199→	if telemetryRaw, ok := raw["telemetry"]; ok {
   200→		var t bool
   201→		if err := json.Unmarshal(telemetryRaw, &t); err != nil {
   202→			return fmt.Errorf("parsing telemetry field: %w", err)
   203→		}
   204→		settings.Telemetry = &t
   205→	}
   206→
   207→	return nil
   208→}
   209→
   210→func applyDefaults(settings *EntireSettings) {
   211→	if settings.Strategy == "" {
   212→		settings.Strategy = DefaultStrategyName
   213→	}
   214→}
   215→
   216→// IsSummarizeEnabled checks if auto-summarize is enabled in settings.
   217→// Returns false by default if settings cannot be loaded or the key is missing.
   218→func IsSummarizeEnabled() bool {
   219→	settings, err := Load()
   220→	if err != nil {
   221→		return false
   222→	}
   223→	return settings.IsSummarizeEnabled()
   224→}
   225→
   226→// IsSummarizeEnabled checks if auto-summarize is enabled in this settings instance.
   227→func (s *EntireSettings) IsSummarizeEnabled() bool {
   228→	if s.StrategyOptions == nil {
   229→		return false
   230→	}
   231→	summarizeOpts, ok := s.StrategyOptions["summarize"].(map[string]any)
   232→	if !ok {
   233→		return false
   234→	}
   235→	enabled, ok := summarizeOpts["enabled"].(bool)
   236→	if !ok {
   237→		return false
   238→	}
   239→	return enabled
   240→}
   241→
   242→// IsPushSessionsDisabled checks if push_sessions is disabled in settings.
   243→// Returns true if push_sessions is explicitly set to false.
   244→func (s *EntireSettings) IsPushSessionsDisabled() bool {
   245→	if s.StrategyOptions == nil {
   246→		return false
   247→	}
   248→	val, exists := s.StrategyOptions["push_sessions"]
   249→	if !exists {
   250→		return false
   251→	}
   252→	if boolVal, ok := val.(bool); ok {
   253→		return !boolVal // disabled = !push_sessions
   254→	}
   255→	return false
   256→}
   257→
   258→// Save saves the settings to .entire/settings.json.
   259→func Save(settings *EntireSettings) error {
   260→	return saveToFile(settings, EntireSettingsFile)
   261→}
   262→
   263→// SaveLocal saves the settings to .entire/settings.local.json.
   264→func SaveLocal(settings *EntireSettings) error {
   265→	return saveToFile(settings, EntireSettingsLocalFile)
   266→}
   267→
   268→// saveToFile saves settings to the specified file path.
   269→func saveToFile(settings *EntireSettings, filePath string) error {
   270→	// Get absolute path for the file
   271→	filePathAbs, err := paths.AbsPath(filePath)
   272→	if err != nil {
   273→		filePathAbs = filePath // Fallback to relative
   274→	}
   275→
   276→	// Ensure directory exists
   277→	dir := filepath.Dir(filePathAbs)
   278→	if err := os.MkdirAll(dir, 0o750); err != nil {
   279→		return fmt.Errorf("creating settings directory: %w", err)
   280→	}
   281→
   282→	data, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(settings, "", "  ")
   283→	if err != nil {
   284→		return fmt.Errorf("marshaling settings: %w", err)
   285→	}
   286→
   287→	//nolint:gosec // G306: settings file is config, not secrets; 0o644 is appropriate
   288→	if err := os.WriteFile(filePathAbs, data, 0o644); err != nil {
   289→		return fmt.Errorf("writing settings file: %w", err)
   290→	}
   291→	return nil
   292→}

     1→package claudecode
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"encoding/json"
     5→	"fmt"
     6→	"os"
     7→	"path/filepath"
     8→	"slices"
     9→	"strings"
    10→
    11→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent"
    12→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/jsonutil"
    13→	"github.com/entireio/cli/cmd/entire/cli/paths"
    14→)
    15→
    16→// Ensure ClaudeCodeAgent implements HookSupport and HookHandler
    17→var (
    18→	_ agent.HookSupport = (*ClaudeCodeAgent)(nil)
    19→	_ agent.HookHandler = (*ClaudeCodeAgent)(nil)
    20→)
    21→
    22→// Claude Code hook names - these become subcommands under `entire hooks claude-code`
    23→const (
    24→	HookNameSessionStart     = "session-start"
    25→	HookNameSessionEnd       = "session-end"
    26→	HookNameStop             = "stop"
    27→	HookNameUserPromptSubmit = "user-prompt-submit"
    28→	HookNamePreTask          = "pre-task"
    29→	HookNamePostTask         = "post-task"
    30→	HookNamePostTodo         = "post-todo"
    31→)
    32→
    33→// ClaudeSettingsFileName is the settings file used by Claude Code.
    34→// This is Claude-specific and not shared with other agents.
    35→const ClaudeSettingsFileName = "settings.json"
    36→
    37→// metadataDenyRule blocks Claude from reading Entire session metadata
    38→const metadataDenyRule = "Read(./.entire/metadata/**)"
    39→
    40→// GetHookNames returns the hook verbs Claude Code supports.
    41→// These become subcommands: entire hooks claude-code <verb>
    42→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetHookNames() []string {
    43→	return []string{
    44→		HookNameSessionStart,
    45→		HookNameSessionEnd,
    46→		HookNameStop,
    47→		HookNameUserPromptSubmit,
    48→		HookNamePreTask,
    49→		HookNamePostTask,
    50→		HookNamePostTodo,
    51→	}
    52→}
    53→
    54→// entireHookPrefixes are command prefixes that identify Entire hooks (both old and new formats)
    55→var entireHookPrefixes = []string{
    56→	"entire ",
    57→	"go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go ",
    58→}
    59→
    60→// InstallHooks installs Claude Code hooks in .claude/settings.json.
    61→// If force is true, removes existing Entire hooks before installing.
    62→// Returns the number of hooks installed.
    63→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error) {
    64→	// Use repo root instead of CWD to find .claude directory
    65→	// This ensures hooks are installed correctly when run from a subdirectory
    66→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    67→	if err != nil {
    68→		// Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo (e.g., during tests)
    69→		repoRoot, err = os.Getwd() //nolint:forbidigo // Intentional fallback when RepoRoot() fails (tests run outside git repos)
    70→		if err != nil {
    71→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to get current directory: %w", err)
    72→		}
    73→	}
    74→
    75→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".claude", ClaudeSettingsFileName)
    76→
    77→	// Read existing settings if they exist
    78→	var settings ClaudeSettings
    79→	var rawSettings map[string]json.RawMessage
    80→
    81→	// rawPermissions preserves unknown permission fields (e.g., "ask")
    82→	var rawPermissions map[string]json.RawMessage
    83→
    84→	existingData, readErr := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from cwd + fixed path
    85→	if readErr == nil {
    86→		if err := json.Unmarshal(existingData, &rawSettings); err != nil {
    87→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse existing settings.json: %w", err)
    88→		}
    89→		if hooksRaw, ok := rawSettings["hooks"]; ok {
    90→			if err := json.Unmarshal(hooksRaw, &settings.Hooks); err != nil {
    91→				return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hooks in settings.json: %w", err)
    92→			}
    93→		}
    94→		if permRaw, ok := rawSettings["permissions"]; ok {
    95→			if err := json.Unmarshal(permRaw, &rawPermissions); err != nil {
    96→				return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse permissions in settings.json: %w", err)
    97→			}
    98→		}
    99→	} else {
   100→		rawSettings = make(map[string]json.RawMessage)
   101→	}
   102→
   103→	if rawPermissions == nil {
   104→		rawPermissions = make(map[string]json.RawMessage)
   105→	}
   106→
   107→	// If force is true, remove all existing Entire hooks first
   108→	if force {
   109→		settings.Hooks.SessionStart = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.SessionStart)
   110→		settings.Hooks.SessionEnd = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.SessionEnd)
   111→		settings.Hooks.Stop = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.Stop)
   112→		settings.Hooks.UserPromptSubmit = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.UserPromptSubmit)
   113→		settings.Hooks.PreToolUse = removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(settings.Hooks.PreToolUse)
   114→		settings.Hooks.PostToolUse = removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(settings.Hooks.PostToolUse)
   115→	}
   116→
   117→	// Define hook commands
   118→	var sessionStartCmd, sessionEndCmd, stopCmd, userPromptSubmitCmd, preTaskCmd, postTaskCmd, postTodoCmd string
   119→	if localDev {
   120→		sessionStartCmd = "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claude-code session-start"
   121→		sessionEndCmd = "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claude-code session-end"
   122→		stopCmd = "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claude-code stop"
   123→		userPromptSubmitCmd = "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claude-code user-prompt-submit"
   124→		preTaskCmd = "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claude-code pre-task"
   125→		postTaskCmd = "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claude-code post-task"
   126→		postTodoCmd = "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claude-code post-todo"
   127→	} else {
   128→		sessionStartCmd = "entire hooks claude-code session-start"
   129→		sessionEndCmd = "entire hooks claude-code session-end"
   130→		stopCmd = "entire hooks claude-code stop"
   131→		userPromptSubmitCmd = "entire hooks claude-code user-prompt-submit"
   132→		preTaskCmd = "entire hooks claude-code pre-task"
   133→		postTaskCmd = "entire hooks claude-code post-task"
   134→		postTodoCmd = "entire hooks claude-code post-todo"
   135→	}
   136→
   137→	count := 0
   138→
   139→	// Add hooks if they don't exist
   140→	if !hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.SessionStart, sessionStartCmd) {
   141→		settings.Hooks.SessionStart = addHookToMatcher(settings.Hooks.SessionStart, "", sessionStartCmd)
   142→		count++
   143→	}
   144→	if !hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.SessionEnd, sessionEndCmd) {
   145→		settings.Hooks.SessionEnd = addHookToMatcher(settings.Hooks.SessionEnd, "", sessionEndCmd)
   146→		count++
   147→	}
   148→	if !hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, stopCmd) {
   149→		settings.Hooks.Stop = addHookToMatcher(settings.Hooks.Stop, "", stopCmd)
   150→		count++
   151→	}
   152→	if !hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.UserPromptSubmit, userPromptSubmitCmd) {
   153→		settings.Hooks.UserPromptSubmit = addHookToMatcher(settings.Hooks.UserPromptSubmit, "", userPromptSubmitCmd)
   154→		count++
   155→	}
   156→	if !hookCommandExistsWithMatcher(settings.Hooks.PreToolUse, "Task", preTaskCmd) {
   157→		settings.Hooks.PreToolUse = addHookToMatcher(settings.Hooks.PreToolUse, "Task", preTaskCmd)
   158→		count++
   159→	}
   160→	if !hookCommandExistsWithMatcher(settings.Hooks.PostToolUse, "Task", postTaskCmd) {
   161→		settings.Hooks.PostToolUse = addHookToMatcher(settings.Hooks.PostToolUse, "Task", postTaskCmd)
   162→		count++
   163→	}
   164→	if !hookCommandExistsWithMatcher(settings.Hooks.PostToolUse, "TodoWrite", postTodoCmd) {
   165→		settings.Hooks.PostToolUse = addHookToMatcher(settings.Hooks.PostToolUse, "TodoWrite", postTodoCmd)
   166→		count++
   167→	}
   168→
   169→	// Add permissions.deny rule if not present
   170→	permissionsChanged := false
   171→	var denyRules []string
   172→	if denyRaw, ok := rawPermissions["deny"]; ok {
   173→		if err := json.Unmarshal(denyRaw, &denyRules); err != nil {
   174→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse permissions.deny in settings.json: %w", err)
   175→		}
   176→	}
   177→	if !slices.Contains(denyRules, metadataDenyRule) {
   178→		denyRules = append(denyRules, metadataDenyRule)
   179→		denyJSON, err := json.Marshal(denyRules)
   180→		if err != nil {
   181→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal permissions.deny: %w", err)
   182→		}
   183→		rawPermissions["deny"] = denyJSON
   184→		permissionsChanged = true
   185→	}
   186→
   187→	if count == 0 && !permissionsChanged {
   188→		return 0, nil // All hooks and permissions already installed
   189→	}
   190→
   191→	// Marshal hooks and update raw settings
   192→	hooksJSON, err := json.Marshal(settings.Hooks)
   193→	if err != nil {
   194→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal hooks: %w", err)
   195→	}
   196→	rawSettings["hooks"] = hooksJSON
   197→
   198→	// Marshal permissions and update raw settings
   199→	permJSON, err := json.Marshal(rawPermissions)
   200→	if err != nil {
   201→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal permissions: %w", err)
   202→	}
   203→	rawSettings["permissions"] = permJSON
   204→
   205→	// Write back to file
   206→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(settingsPath), 0o750); err != nil {
   207→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create .claude directory: %w", err)
   208→	}
   209→
   210→	output, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(rawSettings, "", "  ")
   211→	if err != nil {
   212→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal settings: %w", err)
   213→	}
   214→
   215→	if err := os.WriteFile(settingsPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {
   216→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to write settings.json: %w", err)
   217→	}
   218→
   219→	return count, nil
   220→}
   221→
   222→// UninstallHooks removes Entire hooks from Claude Code settings.
   223→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) UninstallHooks() error {
   224→	// Use repo root to find .claude directory when run from a subdirectory
   225→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   226→	if err != nil {
   227→		repoRoot = "." // Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo
   228→	}
   229→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".claude", ClaudeSettingsFileName)
   230→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   231→	if err != nil {
   232→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // No settings file means nothing to uninstall
   233→	}
   234→
   235→	var rawSettings map[string]json.RawMessage
   236→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &rawSettings); err != nil {
   237→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse settings.json: %w", err)
   238→	}
   239→
   240→	var settings ClaudeSettings
   241→	if hooksRaw, ok := rawSettings["hooks"]; ok {
   242→		if err := json.Unmarshal(hooksRaw, &settings.Hooks); err != nil {
   243→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hooks: %w", err)
   244→		}
   245→	}
   246→
   247→	// Remove Entire hooks from all hook types
   248→	settings.Hooks.SessionStart = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.SessionStart)
   249→	settings.Hooks.SessionEnd = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.SessionEnd)
   250→	settings.Hooks.Stop = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.Stop)
   251→	settings.Hooks.UserPromptSubmit = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.UserPromptSubmit)
   252→	settings.Hooks.PreToolUse = removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(settings.Hooks.PreToolUse)
   253→	settings.Hooks.PostToolUse = removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(settings.Hooks.PostToolUse)
   254→
   255→	// Also remove the metadata deny rule from permissions
   256→	var rawPermissions map[string]json.RawMessage
   257→	if permRaw, ok := rawSettings["permissions"]; ok {
   258→		if err := json.Unmarshal(permRaw, &rawPermissions); err != nil {
   259→			// If parsing fails, just skip permissions cleanup
   260→			rawPermissions = nil
   261→		}
   262→	}
   263→
   264→	if rawPermissions != nil {
   265→		if denyRaw, ok := rawPermissions["deny"]; ok {
   266→			var denyRules []string
   267→			if err := json.Unmarshal(denyRaw, &denyRules); err == nil {
   268→				// Filter out the metadata deny rule
   269→				filteredRules := make([]string, 0, len(denyRules))
   270→				for _, rule := range denyRules {
   271→					if rule != metadataDenyRule {
   272→						filteredRules = append(filteredRules, rule)
   273→					}
   274→				}
   275→				if len(filteredRules) > 0 {
   276→					denyJSON, err := json.Marshal(filteredRules)
   277→					if err == nil {
   278→						rawPermissions["deny"] = denyJSON
   279→					}
   280→				} else {
   281→					// Remove empty deny array
   282→					delete(rawPermissions, "deny")
   283→				}
   284→			}
   285→		}
   286→
   287→		// If permissions is empty, remove it entirely
   288→		if len(rawPermissions) > 0 {
   289→			permJSON, err := json.Marshal(rawPermissions)
   290→			if err == nil {
   291→				rawSettings["permissions"] = permJSON
   292→			}
   293→		} else {
   294→			delete(rawSettings, "permissions")
   295→		}
   296→	}
   297→
   298→	// Marshal hooks back
   299→	hooksJSON, err := json.Marshal(settings.Hooks)
   300→	if err != nil {
   301→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal hooks: %w", err)
   302→	}
   303→	rawSettings["hooks"] = hooksJSON
   304→
   305→	// Write back
   306→	output, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(rawSettings, "", "  ")
   307→	if err != nil {
   308→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal settings: %w", err)
   309→	}
   310→	if err := os.WriteFile(settingsPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {
   311→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write settings.json: %w", err)
   312→	}
   313→	return nil
   314→}
   315→
   316→// AreHooksInstalled checks if Entire hooks are installed.
   317→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) AreHooksInstalled() bool {
   318→	// Use repo root to find .claude directory when run from a subdirectory
   319→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   320→	if err != nil {
   321→		repoRoot = "." // Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo
   322→	}
   323→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".claude", ClaudeSettingsFileName)
   324→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   325→	if err != nil {
   326→		return false
   327→	}
   328→
   329→	var settings ClaudeSettings
   330→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &settings); err != nil {
   331→		return false
   332→	}
   333→
   334→	// Check for at least one of our hooks (new or old format)
   335→	return hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "entire hooks claude-code stop") ||
   336→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claude-code stop") ||
   337→		// Backwards compatibility: check for old hook formats
   338→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "entire hooks claudecode stop") ||
   339→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claudecode stop") ||
   340→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "entire rewind claude-hook --stop") ||
   341→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go rewind claude-hook --stop")
   342→}
   343→
   344→// GetSupportedHooks returns the hook types Claude Code supports.
   345→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetSupportedHooks() []agent.HookType {
   346→	return []agent.HookType{
   347→		agent.HookSessionStart,
   348→		agent.HookSessionEnd,
   349→		agent.HookUserPromptSubmit,
   350→		agent.HookStop,
   351→		agent.HookPreToolUse,
   352→		agent.HookPostToolUse,
   353→	}
   354→}
   355→
   356→// Helper functions for hook management
   357→
   358→func hookCommandExists(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher, command string) bool {
   359→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   360→		for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   361→			if hook.Command == command {
   362→				return true
   363→			}
   364→		}
   365→	}
   366→	return false
   367→}
   368→
   369→func hookCommandExistsWithMatcher(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher, matcherName, command string) bool {
   370→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   371→		if matcher.Matcher == matcherName {
   372→			for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   373→				if hook.Command == command {
   374→					return true
   375→				}
   376→			}
   377→		}
   378→	}
   379→	return false
   380→}
   381→
   382→func addHookToMatcher(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher, matcherName, command string) []ClaudeHookMatcher {
   383→	entry := ClaudeHookEntry{
   384→		Type:    "command",
   385→		Command: command,
   386→	}
   387→
   388→	// If no matcher name, add to a matcher with empty string
   389→	if matcherName == "" {
   390→		for i, matcher := range matchers {
   391→			if matcher.Matcher == "" {
   392→				matchers[i].Hooks = append(matchers[i].Hooks, entry)
   393→				return matchers
   394→			}
   395→		}
   396→		return append(matchers, ClaudeHookMatcher{
   397→			Matcher: "",
   398→			Hooks:   []ClaudeHookEntry{entry},
   399→		})
   400→	}
   401→
   402→	// Find or create matcher with the given name
   403→	for i, matcher := range matchers {
   404→		if matcher.Matcher == matcherName {
   405→			matchers[i].Hooks = append(matchers[i].Hooks, entry)
   406→			return matchers
   407→		}
   408→	}
   409→
   410→	return append(matchers, ClaudeHookMatcher{
   411→		Matcher: matcherName,
   412→		Hooks:   []ClaudeHookEntry{entry},
   413→	})
   414→}
   415→
   416→// isEntireHook checks if a command is an Entire hook (old or new format)
   417→func isEntireHook(command string) bool {
   418→	for _, prefix := range entireHookPrefixes {
   419→		if strings.HasPrefix(command, prefix) {
   420→			return true
   421→		}
   422→	}
   423→	return false
   424→}
   425→
   426→// removeEntireHooks removes all Entire hooks from a list of matchers (for simple hooks like Stop)
   427→func removeEntireHooks(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher) []ClaudeHookMatcher {
   428→	result := make([]ClaudeHookMatcher, 0, len(matchers))
   429→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   430→		filteredHooks := make([]ClaudeHookEntry, 0, len(matcher.Hooks))
   431→		for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   432→			if !isEntireHook(hook.Command) {
   433→				filteredHooks = append(filteredHooks, hook)
   434→			}
   435→		}
   436→		// Only keep the matcher if it has hooks remaining
   437→		if len(filteredHooks) > 0 {
   438→			matcher.Hooks = filteredHooks
   439→			result = append(result, matcher)
   440→		}
   441→	}
   442→	return result
   443→}
   444→
   445→// removeEntireHooksFromMatchers removes Entire hooks from tool-use matchers (PreToolUse, PostToolUse)
   446→// This handles the nested structure where hooks are grouped by tool matcher (e.g., "Task", "TodoWrite")
   447→func removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher) []ClaudeHookMatcher {
   448→	// Same logic as removeEntireHooks - both work on the same structure
   449→	return removeEntireHooks(matchers)
   450→}
   451→

> AGENT

Looking at the code, I found an issue. The `.claude/settings.json` loading in `hooks.go` **does** lose unknown hook types.

Here's the problem in `InstallHooks` (`cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/hooks.go`):

```go
// Line 79-92: Reads hooks into typed struct
var settings ClaudeSettings
if hooksRaw, ok := rawSettings["hooks"]; ok {
    if err := json.Unmarshal(hooksRaw, &settings.Hooks); err != nil {
        return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hooks in settings.json: %w", err)
    }
}
```

And the `ClaudeHooks` struct in `types.go:10-18` only defines 6 hook types:

```go
type ClaudeHooks struct {
    SessionStart     []ClaudeHookMatcher `json:"SessionStart,omitempty"`
    SessionEnd       []ClaudeHookMatcher `json:"SessionEnd,omitempty"`
    UserPromptSubmit []ClaudeHookMatcher `json:"UserPromptSubmit,omitempty"`
    Stop             []ClaudeHookMatcher `json:"Stop,omitempty"`
    PreToolUse       []ClaudeHookMatcher `json:"PreToolUse,omitempty"`
    PostToolUse      []ClaudeHookMatcher `json:"PostToolUse,omitempty"`
}
```

When the code writes back (line 192-196):
```go
hooksJSON, err := json.Marshal(settings.Hooks)
// ...
rawSettings["hooks"] = hooksJSON
```

**Any hook types not in this struct (e.g., `Notification`, `PreFileEdit`, future hooks) are silently dropped** when the settings are saved.

The same issue exists in `UninstallHooks()` (line 240-243, 298-303).

Note: The code *does* preserve unknown **top-level** fields via `rawSettings map[string]json.RawMessage`, and unknown **permission** fields via `rawPermissions`. It's only the hooks object that loses unknown keys.

Would you like me to fix this by using a similar `map[string]json.RawMessage` approach for the hooks section?

> DEVELOPER

and other unknown top level fields?

> AGENT

Good question. Looking at the code again:

**Top-level fields are preserved** because both functions use `map[string]json.RawMessage`:

```go
// InstallHooks line 79, UninstallHooks line 235
var rawSettings map[string]json.RawMessage
if err := json.Unmarshal(existingData, &rawSettings); err != nil { ... }
```

And write the whole map back:
```go
// InstallHooks line 210, UninstallHooks line 306
output, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(rawSettings, "", "  ")
```

So if `.claude/settings.json` has fields like `"apiConfiguration"`, `"model"`, `"customInstructions"`, etc., those are preserved.

**Summary:**

| Level | Preserved? |
|-------|------------|
| Unknown top-level fields (e.g., `model`, `apiConfiguration`) | ✓ Yes |
| Unknown permission fields (e.g., `ask`, `allow`) | ✓ Yes |
| Unknown hook types (e.g., `Notification`, `PreFileEdit`) | ✗ **No** |

The hooks object is the only place where unknown keys get dropped, because it's unmarshaled into the typed `ClaudeHooks` struct rather than a `map[string]json.RawMessage`.

> DEVELOPER

ok, I think we should change that to not be strict, we don't want to play catch up and add new hook types constantly so I rather would like to have something more flexible

> AGENT

That makes sense. I'll update the hooks handling to use a flexible `map[string]json.RawMessage` approach, similar to how top-level fields and permissions are already handled.

{
  "file_path": "/Users/soph/.claude/plans/glittery-snacking-raccoon.md",
  "content": "# Plan: Preserve Unknown Hook Types in .claude/settings.json\n\n## Problem\nWhen `InstallHooks` or `UninstallHooks` modifies `.claude/settings.json`, any hook types not defined in the `ClaudeHooks` struct are silently dropped. This means if Claude Code adds new hook types (e.g., `Notification`, `PreFileEdit`), they'll be lost.\n\n## Solution\nChange from using a typed `ClaudeHooks` struct to a `map[string]json.RawMessage` approach for the hooks object, similar to how top-level fields and permissions are already handled.\n\n## Files to Modify\n\n### 1. `cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/hooks.go`\n\n**InstallHooks function:**\n- Replace `var settings ClaudeSettings` with `var rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage`\n- Parse only the hook types we need to modify (SessionStart, SessionEnd, Stop, UserPromptSubmit, PreToolUse, PostToolUse) from the raw map\n- After modifying, marshal each back into the rawHooks map\n- Write rawHooks back to rawSettings[\"hooks\"]\n\n**UninstallHooks function:**\n- Same approach: use `map[string]json.RawMessage` for hooks\n- Only parse/modify the hook types we care about\n- Preserve all other hook types\n\n**AreHooksInstalled function:**\n- Can keep using the typed struct since it only reads (doesn't write back)\n- Or change for consistency - optional\n\n### 2. `cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/types.go`\n\n- Keep `ClaudeHooks` struct for use in `AreHooksInstalled` (read-only)\n- Or remove if we change that function too\n\n## Implementation Approach\n\n```go\n// Instead of:\nvar settings ClaudeSettings\nif hooksRaw, ok := rawSettings[\"hooks\"]; ok {\n    json.Unmarshal(hooksRaw, &settings.Hooks)\n}\n\n// Use:\nvar rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage\nif hooksRaw, ok := rawSettings[\"hooks\"]; ok {\n    json.Unmarshal(hooksRaw, &rawHooks)\n}\nif rawHooks == nil {\n    rawHooks = make(map[string]json.RawMessage)\n}\n\n// Parse only what we need:\nvar sessionStart []ClaudeHookMatcher\nif data, ok := rawHooks[\"SessionStart\"]; ok {\n    json.Unmarshal(data, &sessionStart)\n}\n// ... same for other hook types we modify\n\n// After modifications, marshal back:\nif len(sessionStart) > 0 {\n    rawHooks[\"SessionStart\"], _ = json.Marshal(sessionStart)\n} else {\n    delete(rawHooks, \"SessionStart\")\n}\n// ... same for others\n\n// Write back to rawSettings\nrawSettings[\"hooks\"], _ = json.Marshal(rawHooks)\n```\n\n## Verification\n\n1. Run existing tests: `mise run test`\n2. Run integration tests: `mise run test:integration`\n3. Manual test:\n   - Create a `.claude/settings.json` with an unknown hook type (e.g., `\"FutureHook\": [...]`)\n   - Run `entire enable --force`\n   - Verify the unknown hook type is preserved\n"
}

Based on the search results, here's what I found about Claude Code hooks documentation:

## Hook Types

**Command hooks** (`type: "command"`): Run a shell command. Your script receives the event's JSON input on stdin and communicates results back through exit codes and stdout.(no content)**Prompt hooks** (`type: "prompt"`): Send a prompt to a Claude model for single-turn evaluation. The model returns a yes/no decision as JSON.(no content)**Agent hooks** (`type: "agent"`): Spawn a subagent that can use tools like Read, Grep, and Glob to verify conditions before returning a decision.

## Hook Events

The available hook events include:
- **PreToolUse** - Before tool execution (can return additionalContext in v2.1.9+)
- **PostToolUse** - After tool completion
- **UserPromptSubmit** - When user submits a prompt
- **PermissionRequest** - When Claude requests permission for a tool (v2.0.45+)
- **Stop** - When Claude Code agent finishes responding
- **SubagentStop** - When a subagent finishes (v1.0.41+)
- **SessionEnd** - When Claude Code session terminates
- **Setup** - Triggered via --init, --init-only, or --maintenance CLI flags (v2.1.10+)

## Settings File Locations

Hooks live in JSON settings files at three levels:
- **Project-level**: `.claude/settings.json` within your repository (shareable with team)
- **User-level**: `~/.claude/settings.json` (applies across all projects)
- **Local project**: `.claude/settings.local.json` (personal config, not committed)

Project-level settings take precedence over user-level settings. Enterprise-managed policy settings are also available for organizational control.

## Hook Configuration Structure

Hooks are configured in your settings files using a JSON structure with event names, matchers (to filter which tools trigger the hook), and the commands to run. They execute in your local environment with your user permissions, receiving information about the triggering event via stdin and communicating back through exit codes and stdout.

## Exit Codes & JSON Output

Exit codes determine the basic outcome:
- **Exit code 0**: Success (stdout gets processed for JSON or added to context)
- **Exit code 2**: Blocking error (stderr becomes the error message, action prevented)
- **Other codes**: Non-blocking errors (stderr shown in verbose mode)

Hooks can return structured JSON with fields including: `decision` (approve, block, allow, or deny), `reason` (explanation shown to Claude), `continue` (for Stop hooks), and `updatedInput` (to modify tool parameters).

## Additional Features

To remove a hook, delete its entry from the settings JSON file or use the `/hooks` menu. To temporarily disable all hooks without removing them, set `"disableAllHooks": true` in your settings file. There is no way to disable an individual hook while keeping it in the configuration.(no content)Hook timeout: Tool hook execution timeout is 10 minutes (increased from 60 seconds in v2.1.3).


REMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks.

## Approved Plan:
# Plan: Preserve Unknown Hook Types in .claude/settings.json

## Problem
When `InstallHooks` or `UninstallHooks` modifies `.claude/settings.json`, any hook types not defined in the `ClaudeHooks` struct are silently dropped. This means if Claude Code adds new hook types (e.g., `Notification`, `PreFileEdit`), they'll be lost.

## Solution
Change from using a typed `ClaudeHooks` struct to a `map[string]json.RawMessage` approach for the hooks object, similar to how top-level fields and permissions are already handled.

## Files to Modify

### 1. `cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/hooks.go`

**InstallHooks function:**
- Replace `var settings ClaudeSettings` with `var rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage`
- Parse only the hook types we need to modify (SessionStart, SessionEnd, Stop, UserPromptSubmit, PreToolUse, PostToolUse) from the raw map
- After modifying, marshal each back into the rawHooks map
- Write rawHooks back to rawSettings["hooks"]

**UninstallHooks function:**
- Same approach: use `map[string]json.RawMessage` for hooks
- Only parse/modify the hook types we care about
- Preserve all other hook types

**AreHooksInstalled function:**
- Can keep using the typed struct since it only reads (doesn't write back)
- Or change for consistency - optional

### 2. `cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/types.go`

- Keep `ClaudeHooks` struct for use in `AreHooksInstalled` (read-only)
- Or remove if we change that function too

## Implementation Approach

```go
// Instead of:
var settings ClaudeSettings
if hooksRaw, ok := rawSettings["hooks"]; ok {
    json.Unmarshal(hooksRaw, &settings.Hooks)
}

// Use:
var rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage
if hooksRaw, ok := rawSettings["hooks"]; ok {
    json.Unmarshal(hooksRaw, &rawHooks)
}
if rawHooks == nil {
    rawHooks = make(map[string]json.RawMessage)
}

// Parse only what we need:
var sessionStart []ClaudeHookMatcher
if data, ok := rawHooks["SessionStart"]; ok {
    json.Unmarshal(data, &sessionStart)
}
// ... same for other hook types we modify

// After modifications, marshal back:
if len(sessionStart) > 0 {
    rawHooks["SessionStart"], _ = json.Marshal(sessionStart)
} else {
    delete(rawHooks, "SessionStart")
}
// ... same for others

// Write back to rawSettings
rawSettings["hooks"], _ = json.Marshal(rawHooks)
```

## Verification

1. Run existing tests: `mise run test`
2. Run integration tests: `mise run test:integration`
3. Add a unit test that:
   - Creates a `.claude/settings.json` with a hook type we don't use (e.g., `Notification`)
   - Runs `InstallHooks`
   - Verifies the `Notification` hook is preserved in the output

Note: Claude Code has several hook types we don't currently handle: `Notification`, `SubagentStop`, `Setup`. Using `Notification` as the test case since it's a real hook type.

Sources:
- [Hooks reference - Claude Code Docs](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks)

```

   126→		postTodoCmd = "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claude-code post-todo"
   127→	} else {
   128→		sessionStartCmd = "entire hooks claude-code session-start"
   129→		sessionEndCmd = "entire hooks claude-code session-end"
   130→		stopCmd = "entire hooks claude-code stop"
   131→		userPromptSubmitCmd = "entire hooks claude-code user-prompt-submit"
   132→		preTaskCmd = "entire hooks claude-code pre-task"
   133→		postTaskCmd = "entire hooks claude-code post-task"
   134→		postTodoCmd = "entire hooks claude-code post-todo"
   135→	}
   136→
   137→	count := 0
   138→
   139→	// Add hooks if they don't exist
   140→	if !hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.SessionStart, sessionStartCmd) {
   141→		settings.Hooks.SessionStart = addHookToMatcher(settings.Hooks.SessionStart, "", sessionStartCmd)
   142→		count++
   143→	}
   144→	if !hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.SessionEnd, sessionEndCmd) {
   145→		settings.Hooks.SessionEnd = addHookToMatcher(settings.Hooks.SessionEnd, "", sessionEndCmd)
   146→		count++
   147→	}
   148→	if !hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, stopCmd) {
   149→		settings.Hooks.Stop = addHookToMatcher(settings.Hooks.Stop, "", stopCmd)
   150→		count++
   151→	}
   152→	if !hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.UserPromptSubmit, userPromptSubmitCmd) {
   153→		settings.Hooks.UserPromptSubmit = addHookToMatcher(settings.Hooks.UserPromptSubmit, "", userPromptSubmitCmd)
   154→		count++
   155→	}
   156→	if !hookCommandExistsWithMatcher(settings.Hooks.PreToolUse, "Task", preTaskCmd) {
   157→		settings.Hooks.PreToolUse = addHookToMatcher(settings.Hooks.PreToolUse, "Task", preTaskCmd)
   158→		count++
   159→	}
   160→	if !hookCommandExistsWithMatcher(settings.Hooks.PostToolUse, "Task", postTaskCmd) {
   161→		settings.Hooks.PostToolUse = addHookToMatcher(settings.Hooks.PostToolUse, "Task", postTaskCmd)
   162→		count++
   163→	}
   164→	if !hookCommandExistsWithMatcher(settings.Hooks.PostToolUse, "TodoWrite", postTodoCmd) {
   165→		settings.Hooks.PostToolUse = addHookToMatcher(settings.Hooks.PostToolUse, "TodoWrite", postTodoCmd)
   166→		count++
   167→	}
   168→
   169→	// Add permissions.deny rule if not present
   170→	permissionsChanged := false
   171→	var denyRules []string
   172→	if denyRaw, ok := rawPermissions["deny"]; ok {
   173→		if err := json.Unmarshal(denyRaw, &denyRules); err != nil {
   174→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse permissions.deny in settings.json: %w", err)
   175→		}
   176→	}
   177→	if !slices.Contains(denyRules, metadataDenyRule) {
   178→		denyRules = append(denyRules, metadataDenyRule)
   179→		denyJSON, err := json.Marshal(denyRules)
   180→		if err != nil {
   181→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal permissions.deny: %w", err)
   182→		}
   183→		rawPermissions["deny"] = denyJSON
   184→		permissionsChanged = true
   185→	}
   186→
   187→	if count == 0 && !permissionsChanged {
   188→		return 0, nil // All hooks and permissions already installed
   189→	}
   190→
   191→	// Marshal hooks and update raw settings
   192→	hooksJSON, err := json.Marshal(settings.Hooks)
   193→	if err != nil {
   194→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal hooks: %w", err)
   195→	}
   196→	rawSettings["hooks"] = hooksJSON
   197→
   198→	// Marshal permissions and update raw settings
   199→	permJSON, err := json.Marshal(rawPermissions)
   200→	if err != nil {
   201→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal permissions: %w", err)
   202→	}
   203→	rawSettings["permissions"] = permJSON
   204→
   205→	// Write back to file
   206→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(settingsPath), 0o750); err != nil {
   207→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create .claude directory: %w", err)
   208→	}
   209→
   210→	output, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(rawSettings, "", "  ")
   211→	if err != nil {
   212→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal settings: %w", err)
   213→	}
   214→
   215→	if err := os.WriteFile(settingsPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {
   216→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to write settings.json: %w", err)
   217→	}
   218→
   219→	return count, nil
   220→}
   221→
   222→// UninstallHooks removes Entire hooks from Claude Code settings.
   223→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) UninstallHooks() error {
   224→	// Use repo root to find .claude directory when run from a subdirectory
   225→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   226→	if err != nil {
   227→		repoRoot = "." // Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo
   228→	}
   229→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".claude", ClaudeSettingsFileName)
   230→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   231→	if err != nil {
   232→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // No settings file means nothing to uninstall
   233→	}
   234→
   235→	var rawSettings map[string]json.RawMessage
   236→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &rawSettings); err != nil {
   237→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse settings.json: %w", err)
   238→	}
   239→
   240→	var settings ClaudeSettings
   241→	if hooksRaw, ok := rawSettings["hooks"]; ok {
   242→		if err := json.Unmarshal(hooksRaw, &settings.Hooks); err != nil {
   243→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hooks: %w", err)
   244→		}
   245→	}
   246→
   247→	// Remove Entire hooks from all hook types
   248→	settings.Hooks.SessionStart = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.SessionStart)
   249→	settings.Hooks.SessionEnd = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.SessionEnd)
   250→	settings.Hooks.Stop = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.Stop)
   251→	settings.Hooks.UserPromptSubmit = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.UserPromptSubmit)
   252→	settings.Hooks.PreToolUse = removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(settings.Hooks.PreToolUse)
   253→	settings.Hooks.PostToolUse = removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(settings.Hooks.PostToolUse)
   254→
   255→	// Also remove the metadata deny rule from permissions
   256→	var rawPermissions map[string]json.RawMessage
   257→	if permRaw, ok := rawSettings["permissions"]; ok {
   258→		if err := json.Unmarshal(permRaw, &rawPermissions); err != nil {
   259→			// If parsing fails, just skip permissions cleanup
   260→			rawPermissions = nil
   261→		}
   262→	}
   263→
   264→	if rawPermissions != nil {
   265→		if denyRaw, ok := rawPermissions["deny"]; ok {
   266→			var denyRules []string
   267→			if err := json.Unmarshal(denyRaw, &denyRules); err == nil {
   268→				// Filter out the metadata deny rule
   269→				filteredRules := make([]string, 0, len(denyRules))
   270→				for _, rule := range denyRules {
   271→					if rule != metadataDenyRule {
   272→						filteredRules = append(filteredRules, rule)
   273→					}
   274→				}
   275→				if len(filteredRules) > 0 {
   276→					denyJSON, err := json.Marshal(filteredRules)
   277→					if err == nil {
   278→						rawPermissions["deny"] = denyJSON
   279→					}
   280→				} else {
   281→					// Remove empty deny array
   282→					delete(rawPermissions, "deny")
   283→				}
   284→			}
   285→		}
   286→
   287→		// If permissions is empty, remove it entirely
   288→		if len(rawPermissions) > 0 {
   289→			permJSON, err := json.Marshal(rawPermissions)
   290→			if err == nil {
   291→				rawSettings["permissions"] = permJSON
   292→			}
   293→		} else {
   294→			delete(rawSettings, "permissions")
   295→		}
   296→	}
   297→
   298→	// Marshal hooks back
   299→	hooksJSON, err := json.Marshal(settings.Hooks)
   300→	if err != nil {
   301→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal hooks: %w", err)
   302→	}
   303→	rawSettings["hooks"] = hooksJSON
   304→
   305→	// Write back
   306→	output, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(rawSettings, "", "  ")
   307→	if err != nil {
   308→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal settings: %w", err)
   309→	}
   310→	if err := os.WriteFile(settingsPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {
   311→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write settings.json: %w", err)
   312→	}
   313→	return nil
   314→}
   315→
   316→// AreHooksInstalled checks if Entire hooks are installed.
   317→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) AreHooksInstalled() bool {
   318→	// Use repo root to find .claude directory when run from a subdirectory
   319→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   320→	if err != nil {
   321→		repoRoot = "." // Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo
   322→	}
   323→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".claude", ClaudeSettingsFileName)
   324→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   325→	if err != nil {
   326→		return false
   327→	}
   328→
   329→	var settings ClaudeSettings
   330→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &settings); err != nil {
   331→		return false
   332→	}
   333→
   334→	// Check for at least one of our hooks (new or old format)
   335→	return hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "entire hooks claude-code stop") ||
   336→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claude-code stop") ||
   337→		// Backwards compatibility: check for old hook formats
   338→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "entire hooks claudecode stop") ||
   339→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claudecode stop") ||
   340→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "entire rewind claude-hook --stop") ||
   341→		hookCommandExists(settings.Hooks.Stop, "go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go rewind claude-hook --stop")
   342→}
   343→
   344→// GetSupportedHooks returns the hook types Claude Code supports.
   345→func (c *ClaudeCodeAgent) GetSupportedHooks() []agent.HookType {
   346→	return []agent.HookType{
   347→		agent.HookSessionStart,
   348→		agent.HookSessionEnd,
   349→		agent.HookUserPromptSubmit,
   350→		agent.HookStop,
   351→		agent.HookPreToolUse,
   352→		agent.HookPostToolUse,
   353→	}
   354→}
   355→
   356→// Helper functions for hook management
   357→
   358→func hookCommandExists(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher, command string) bool {
   359→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   360→		for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   361→			if hook.Command == command {
   362→				return true
   363→			}
   364→		}
   365→	}
   366→	return false
   367→}
   368→
   369→func hookCommandExistsWithMatcher(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher, matcherName, command string) bool {
   370→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   371→		if matcher.Matcher == matcherName {
   372→			for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   373→				if hook.Command == command {
   374→					return true
   375→				}
   376→			}
   377→		}
   378→	}
   379→	return false
   380→}
   381→
   382→func addHookToMatcher(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher, matcherName, command string) []ClaudeHookMatcher {
   383→	entry := ClaudeHookEntry{
   384→		Type:    "command",
   385→		Command: command,
   386→	}
   387→
   388→	// If no matcher name, add to a matcher with empty string
   389→	if matcherName == "" {
   390→		for i, matcher := range matchers {
   391→			if matcher.Matcher == "" {
   392→				matchers[i].Hooks = append(matchers[i].Hooks, entry)
   393→				return matchers
   394→			}
   395→		}
   396→		return append(matchers, ClaudeHookMatcher{
   397→			Matcher: "",
   398→			Hooks:   []ClaudeHookEntry{entry},
   399→		})
   400→	}
   401→
   402→	// Find or create matcher with the given name
   403→	for i, matcher := range matchers {
   404→		if matcher.Matcher == matcherName {
   405→			matchers[i].Hooks = append(matchers[i].Hooks, entry)
   406→			return matchers
   407→		}
   408→	}
   409→
   410→	return append(matchers, ClaudeHookMatcher{
   411→		Matcher: matcherName,
   412→		Hooks:   []ClaudeHookEntry{entry},
   413→	})
   414→}
   415→
   416→// isEntireHook checks if a command is an Entire hook (old or new format)
   417→func isEntireHook(command string) bool {
   418→	for _, prefix := range entireHookPrefixes {
   419→		if strings.HasPrefix(command, prefix) {
   420→			return true
   421→		}
   422→	}
   423→	return false
   424→}
   425→
   426→// removeEntireHooks removes all Entire hooks from a list of matchers (for simple hooks like Stop)
   427→func removeEntireHooks(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher) []ClaudeHookMatcher {
   428→	result := make([]ClaudeHookMatcher, 0, len(matchers))
   429→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   430→		filteredHooks := make([]ClaudeHookEntry, 0, len(matcher.Hooks))
   431→		for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   432→			if !isEntireHook(hook.Command) {
   433→				filteredHooks = append(filteredHooks, hook)
   434→			}
   435→		}
   436→		// Only keep the matcher if it has hooks remaining
   437→		if len(filteredHooks) > 0 {
   438→			matcher.Hooks = filteredHooks
   439→			result = append(result, matcher)
   440→		}
   441→	}
   442→	return result
   443→}
   444→
   445→// removeEntireHooksFromMatchers removes Entire hooks from tool-use matchers (PreToolUse, PostToolUse)
   446→// This handles the nested structure where hooks are grouped by tool matcher (e.g., "Task", "TodoWrite")
   447→func removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher) []ClaudeHookMatcher {
   448→	// Same logic as removeEntireHooks - both work on the same structure
   449→	return removeEntireHooks(matchers)
   450→}

//nolint:forbidigo // Intentional fallback when RepoRoot() fails (tests run outside git repos)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to get current directory: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tsettingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, \".claude\", ClaudeSettingsFileName)\n\n\t// Read existing settings if they exist\n\tvar rawSettings map[string]json.RawMessage\n\n\t// rawHooks preserves unknown hook types (e.g., \"Notification\", \"SubagentStop\")\n\tvar rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage\n\n\t// rawPermissions preserves unknown permission fields (e.g., \"ask\")\n\tvar rawPermissions map[string]json.RawMessage\n\n\texistingData, readErr := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from cwd + fixed path\n\tif readErr == nil {\n\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(existingData, &rawSettings); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse existing settings.json: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tif hooksRaw, ok := rawSettings[\"hooks\"]; ok {\n\t\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(hooksRaw, &rawHooks); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse hooks in settings.json: %w\", err)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tif permRaw, ok := rawSettings[\"permissions\"]; ok {\n\t\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(permRaw, &rawPermissions); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse permissions in settings.json: %w\", err)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t} else {\n\t\trawSettings = make(map[string]json.RawMessage)\n\t}\n\n\tif rawHooks == nil {\n\t\trawHooks = make(map[string]json.RawMessage)\n\t}\n\tif rawPermissions == nil {\n\t\trawPermissions = make(map[string]json.RawMessage)\n\t}\n\n\t// Parse only the hook types we need to modify\n\tvar sessionStart, sessionEnd, stop, userPromptSubmit, preToolUse, postToolUse []ClaudeHookMatcher\n\tparseHookType(rawHooks, \"SessionStart\", &sessionStart)\n\tparseHookType(rawHooks, \"SessionEnd\", &sessionEnd)\n\tparseHookType(rawHooks, \"Stop\", &stop)\n\tparseHookType(rawHooks, \"UserPromptSubmit\", &userPromptSubmit)\n\tparseHookType(rawHooks, \"PreToolUse\", &preToolUse)\n\tparseHookType(rawHooks, \"PostToolUse\", &postToolUse)\n\n\t// If force is true, remove all existing Entire hooks first\n\tif force {\n\t\tsessionStart = removeEntireHooks(sessionStart)\n\t\tsessionEnd = removeEntireHooks(sessionEnd)\n\t\tstop = removeEntireHooks(stop)\n\t\tuserPromptSubmit = removeEntireHooks(userPromptSubmit)\n\t\tpreToolUse = removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(preToolUse)\n\t\tpostToolUse = removeEntireHooksFromMatchers(postToolUse)\n\t}\n\n\t// Define hook commands\n\tvar sessionStartCmd, sessionEndCmd, stopCmd, userPromptSubmitCmd, preTaskCmd, postTaskCmd, postTodoCmd string\n\tif localDev {\n\t\tsessionStartCmd = \"go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claude-code session-start\"\n\t\tsessionEndCmd = \"go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claude-code session-end\"\n\t\tstopCmd = \"go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claude-code stop\"\n\t\tuserPromptSubmitCmd = \"go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claude-code user-prompt-submit\"\n\t\tpreTaskCmd = \"go run ${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks claude-code post-todo\"\n\t} else {\n\t\tsessionStartCmd = \"entire hooks claude-code session-start\"\n\t\tsessionEndCmd = \"entire hooks claude-code session-end\"\n\t\tstopCmd = \"entire hooks claude-code stop\"\n\t\tuserPromptSubmitCmd = \"entire hooks claude-code user-prompt-submit\"\n\t\tpreTaskCmd = \"entire hooks claude-code pre-task\"\n\t\tpostTaskCmd = \"entire hooks claude-code post-task\"\n\t\tpostTodoCmd = \"entire hooks claude-code post-todo\"\n\t}\n\n\tcount := 0\n\n\t// Add hooks if they don't exist\n\tif !hookCommandExists(sessionStart, sessionStartCmd) {\n\t\tsessionStart = addHookToMatcher(sessionStart, \"\", sessionStartCmd)\n\t\tcount++\n\t}\n\tif !hookCommandExists(sessionEnd, sessionEndCmd) {\n\t\tsessionEnd = addHookToMatcher(sessionEnd, \"\", sessionEndCmd)\n\t\tcount++\n\t}\n\tif !hookCommandExists(stop, stopCmd) {\n\t\tstop = addHookToMatcher(stop, \"\", stopCmd)\n\t\tcount++\n\t}\n\tif !hookCommandExists(userPromptSubmit, userPromptSubmitCmd) {\n\t\tuserPromptSubmit = addHookToMatcher(userPromptSubmit, \"\", userPromptSubmitCmd)\n\t\tcount++\n\t}\n\tif !hookCommandExistsWithMatcher(preToolUse, \"Task\", preTaskCmd) {\n\t\tpreToolUse = addHookToMatcher(preToolUse, \"Task\", preTaskCmd)\n\t\tcount++\n\t}\n\tif !hookCommandExistsWithMatcher(postToolUse, \"Task\", postTaskCmd) {\n\t\tpostToolUse = addHookToMatcher(postToolUse, \"Task\", postTaskCmd)\n\t\tcount++\n\t}\n\tif !hookCommandExistsWithMatcher(postToolUse, \"TodoWrite\", postTodoCmd) {\n\t\tpostToolUse = addHookToMatcher(postToolUse, \"TodoWrite\", postTodoCmd)\n\t\tcount++\n\t}\n\n\t// Add permissions.deny rule if not present\n\tpermissionsChanged := false\n\tvar denyRules []string\n\tif denyRaw, ok := rawPermissions[\"deny\"]; ok {\n\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(denyRaw, &denyRules); err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to parse permissions.deny in settings.json: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif !slices.Contains(denyRules, metadataDenyRule) {\n\t\tdenyRules = append(denyRules, metadataDenyRule)\n\t\tdenyJSON, err := json.Marshal(denyRules)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to marshal permissions.deny: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\trawPermissions[\"deny\"] = denyJSON\n\t\tpermissionsChanged = true\n\t}\n\n\tif count == 0 && !permissionsChanged {\n\t\treturn 0, nil // All hooks and permissions already installed\n\t}\n\n\t// Marshal modified hook types back to rawHooks\n\tmarshalHookType(rawHooks, \"SessionStart\", sessionStart)\n\tmarshalHookType(rawHooks, \"SessionEnd\", sessionEnd)\n\tmarshalHookType(rawHooks, \"Stop\", stop)\n\tmarshalHookType(rawHooks, \"UserPromptSubmit\", userPromptSubmit)\n\tmarshalHookType(rawHooks, \"PreToolUse\", preToolUse)\n\tmarshalHookType(rawHooks, \"PostToolUse\", postToolUse)\n\n\t// Marshal hooks and update raw settings\n\thooksJSON, err := json.Marshal(rawHooks)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to marshal hooks: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\trawSettings[\"hooks\"] = hooksJSON\n\n\t// Marshal permissions and update raw settings\n\tpermJSON, err := json.Marshal(rawPermissions)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to marshal permissions: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\trawSettings[\"permissions\"] = permJSON\n\n\t// Write back to file\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(settingsPath), 0o750); err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to create .claude directory: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\toutput, err := jsonutil.MarshalIndentWithNewline(rawSettings, \"\", \"  \")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to marshal settings: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(settingsPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {\n\t\treturn 0, fmt.Errorf(\"failed to write settings.json: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\treturn count, nil\n}\n\n// parseHookType parses a specific hook type from rawHooks into the target slice.\n// Silently ignores parse errors (leaves target unchanged).\nfunc parseHookType(rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage, hookType string, target *[]ClaudeHookMatcher) {\n\tif data, ok := rawHooks[hookType]; ok {\n\t\t_ = json.Unmarshal(data, target) // Ignore errors, leave target as nil/empty\n\t}\n}\n\n// marshalHookType marshals a hook type back to rawHooks.\n// If the slice is empty, removes the key from rawHooks.\nfunc marshalHookType(rawHooks map[string]json.RawMessage, hookType string, matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher) {\n\tif len(matchers) == 0 {\n\t\tdelete(rawHooks, hookType)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tdata, err := json.Marshal(matchers)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn // Silently ignore marshal errors (shouldn't happen)\n\t}\n\trawHooks[hookType] = data\n}"
}

    13→func TestInstallHooks_PermissionsDeny_FreshInstall(t *testing.T) {

   119→}
   120→
   121→func TestInstallHooks_PermissionsDeny_SkipsExistingRule(t *testing.T) {
   122→	tempDir := t.TempDir()
   123→	t.Chdir(tempDir)
   124→
   125→	// Create settings.json with the rule already present
   126→	writeSettingsFile(t, tempDir, `{
   127→  "permissions": {
   128→    "deny": ["Read(./.entire/metadata/**)"]
   129→  }
   130→}`)
   131→
   132→	agent := &ClaudeCodeAgent{}
   133→	_, err := agent.InstallHooks(false, false)
   134→	if err != nil {

   136→	}

   139→
   140→	// Should still have exactly 1 rule

   146→func TestInstallHooks_PermissionsDeny_PreservesUnknownFields(t *testing.T) {
   147→	tempDir := t.TempDir()
   148→	t.Chdir(tempDir)
   149→
   150→	// Create settings.json with unknown permission fields like "ask"
   151→	writeSettingsFile(t, tempDir, `{
   152→  "permissions": {
   153→    "allow": ["Read(**)"],
   154→    "ask": ["Write(**)", "Bash(*)"],
   155→    "customField": {"nested": "value"}
   156→  }
   157→}`)
   158→
   159→	agent := &ClaudeCodeAgent{}
   160→	_, err := agent.InstallHooks(false, false)
   161→	if err != nil {
   162→		t.Fatalf("InstallHooks() error = %v", err)
   163→	}
   164→
   165→	// Read raw settings to check for unknown fields
   166→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(tempDir, ".claude", "settings.json")
   167→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath)
   168→	if err != nil {
   169→		t.Fatalf("failed to read settings.json: %v", err)
   170→	}
   171→
   172→	var rawSettings map[string]json.RawMessage
   173→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &rawSettings); err != nil {
   174→		t.Fatalf("failed to parse settings.json: %v", err)
   175→	}
   176→
   177→	var rawPermissions map[string]json.RawMessage
   178→	if err := json.Unmarshal(rawSettings["permissions"], &rawPermissions); err != nil {
   179→		t.Fatalf("failed to parse permissions: %v", err)
   180→	}
   181→
   182→	// Verify "ask" field is preserved
   183→	if _, ok := rawPermissions["ask"]; !ok {
   184→		t.Errorf("permissions.ask was not preserved, got keys: %v", getKeys(rawPermissions))
   185→	}
   186→
   187→	// Verify "customField" is preserved
   188→	if _, ok := rawPermissions["customField"]; !ok {
   189→		t.Errorf("permissions.customField was not preserved, got keys: %v", getKeys(rawPermissions))
   190→	}
   191→
   192→	// Verify the "ask" field content
   193→	var askRules []string
   194→	if err := json.Unmarshal(rawPermissions["ask"], &askRules); err != nil {
   195→		t.Fatalf("failed to parse permissions.ask: %v", err)
   196→	}
   197→	if len(askRules) != 2 || askRules[0] != "Write(**)" || askRules[1] != "Bash(*)" {
   198→		t.Errorf("permissions.ask = %v, want [Write(**), Bash(*)]", askRules)
   199→	}
   200→
   201→	// Verify the deny rule was added
   202→	var denyRules []string
   203→	if err := json.Unmarshal(rawPermissions["deny"], &denyRules); err != nil {
   204→		t.Fatalf("failed to parse permissions.deny: %v", err)
   205→	}
   206→	if !containsDenyRule(denyRules, metadataDenyRuleTest) {
   207→		t.Errorf("permissions.deny = %v, want to contain %q", denyRules, metadataDenyRuleTest)
   208→	}
   209→
   210→	// Verify "allow" is preserved
   211→	var allowRules []string
   212→	if err := json.Unmarshal(rawPermissions["allow"], &allowRules); err != nil {
   213→		t.Fatalf("failed to parse permissions.allow: %v", err)
   214→	}
   215→	if len(allowRules) != 1 || allowRules[0] != "Read(**)" {
   216→		t.Errorf("permissions.allow = %v, want [Read(**)]", allowRules)
   217→	}
   218→}
   219→
   220→// Helper functions
   221→
   222→// testPermissions is used only for test assertions
   223→type testPermissions struct {
   224→	Allow []string `json:"allow,omitempty"`
   225→	Deny  []string `json:"deny,omitempty"`
   226→}
   227→
   228→func readPermissions(t *testing.T, tempDir string) testPermissions {
   229→	t.Helper()
   230→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(tempDir, ".claude", "settings.json")
   231→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath)
   232→	if err != nil {
   233→		t.Fatalf("failed to read settings.json: %v", err)
   234→	}
   235→
   236→	var rawSettings map[string]json.RawMessage
   237→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &rawSettings); err != nil {
   238→		t.Fatalf("failed to parse settings.json: %v", err)
   239→	}
   240→
   241→	var perms testPermissions
   242→	if permRaw, ok := rawSettings["permissions"]; ok {
   243→		if err := json.Unmarshal(permRaw, &perms); err != nil {
   244→			t.Fatalf("failed to parse permissions: %v", err)
   245→		}
   246→	}
   247→	return perms
   248→}
   249→
   250→func writeSettingsFile(t *testing.T, tempDir, content string) {
   251→	t.Helper()
   252→	claudeDir := filepath.Join(tempDir, ".claude")
   253→	if err := os.MkdirAll(claudeDir, 0o755); err != nil {
   254→		t.Fatalf("failed to create .claude dir: %v", err)
   255→	}
   256→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(claudeDir, "settings.json")
   257→	if err := os.WriteFile(settingsPath, []byte(content), 0o644); err != nil {
   258→		t.Fatalf("failed to write settings.json: %v", err)
   259→	}
   260→}
   261→
   262→func containsDenyRule(rules []string, rule string) bool {
   263→	for _, r := range rules {
   264→		if r == rule {
   265→			return true
   266→		}
   267→	}
   268→	return false
   269→}
   270→
   271→func getKeys(m map[string]json.RawMessage) []string {
   272→	keys := make([]string, 0, len(m))
   273→	for k := range m {
   274→		keys = append(keys, k)
   275→	}
   276→	return keys
   277→}
   278→
   279→func TestUninstallHooks(t *testing.T) {
   280→	tempDir := t.TempDir()
   281→	t.Chdir(tempDir)
   282→
   283→	agent := &ClaudeCodeAgent{}
   284→
   285→	// First install
   286→	_, err := agent.InstallHooks(false, false)
   287→	if err != nil {
   288→		t.Fatalf("InstallHooks() error = %v", err)
   289→	}
   290→
   291→	// Verify hooks are installed
   292→	if !agent.AreHooksInstalled() {
   293→		t.Error("hooks should be installed before uninstall")
   294→	}
   295→
   296→	// Uninstall
   297→	err = agent.UninstallHooks()
   298→	if err != nil {
   299→		t.Fatalf("UninstallHooks() error = %v", err)
   300→	}
   301→
   302→	// Verify hooks are removed
   303→	if agent.AreHooksInstalled() {
   304→		t.Error("hooks should not be installed after uninstall")
   305→	}
   306→}
   307→
   308→func TestUninstallHooks_NoSettingsFile(t *testing.T) {
   309→	tempDir := t.TempDir()
   310→	t.Chdir(tempDir)
   311→
   312→	agent := &ClaudeCodeAgent{}
   313→
   314→	// Should not error when no settings file exists
   315→	err := agent.UninstallHooks()
   316→	if err != nil {
   317→		t.Fatalf("UninstallHooks() should not error when no settings file: %v", err)
   318→	}
   319→}
   320→
   321→func TestUninstallHooks_PreservesUserHooks(t *testing.T) {
   322→	tempDir := t.TempDir()
   323→	t.Chdir(tempDir)
   324→
   325→	// Create settings with both user and entire hooks
   326→	writeSettingsFile(t, tempDir, `{
   327→  "hooks": {
   328→    "Stop": [
   329→      {
   330→        "matcher": "",
   331→        "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "echo user hook"}]
   332→      },
   333→      {
   334→        "matcher": "",
   335→        "hooks": [{"type": "command", "command": "entire hooks claude-code stop"}]
   336→      }
   337→    ]
   338→  }
   339→}`)
   340→
   341→	agent := &ClaudeCodeAgent{}
   342→	err := agent.UninstallHooks()
   343→	if err != nil {
   344→		t.Fatalf("UninstallHooks() error = %v", err)
   345→	}
   346→
   347→	settings := readClaudeSettings(t, tempDir)
   348→
   349→	// Verify only user hooks remain
   350→	if len(settings.Hooks.Stop) != 1 {
   351→		t.Errorf("Stop hooks = %d after uninstall, want 1 (user only)", len(settings.Hooks.Stop))
   352→	}
   353→
   354→	// Verify it's the user hook
   355→	if len(settings.Hooks.Stop) > 0 && len(settings.Hooks.Stop[0].Hooks) > 0 {
   356→		if settings.Hooks.Stop[0].Hooks[0].Command != "echo user hook" {
   357→			t.Error("user hook was removed during uninstall")
   358→		}
   359→	}
   360→}
   361→
   362→func TestUninstallHooks_RemovesDenyRule(t *testing.T) {
   363→	tempDir := t.TempDir()
   364→	t.Chdir(tempDir)
   365→
   366→	agent := &ClaudeCodeAgent{}
   367→
   368→	// First install (which adds the deny rule)
   369→	_, err := agent.InstallHooks(false, false)
   370→	if err != nil {
   371→		t.Fatalf("InstallHooks() error = %v", err)
   372→	}
   373→
   374→	// Verify deny rule was added
   375→	perms := readPermissions(t, tempDir)
   376→	if !containsDenyRule(perms.Deny, metadataDenyRuleTest) {
   377→		t.Fatal("deny rule should be present after install")
   378→	}
   379→
   380→	// Uninstall
   381→	err = agent.UninstallHooks()
   382→	if err != nil {
   383→		t.Fatalf("UninstallHooks() error = %v", err)
   384→	}
   385→
   386→	// Verify deny rule was removed
   387→	perms = readPermissions(t, tempDir)
   388→	if containsDenyRule(perms.Deny, metadataDenyRuleTest) {
   389→		t.Error("deny rule should be removed after uninstall")
   390→	}
   391→}
   392→
   393→func TestUninstallHooks_PreservesUserDenyRules(t *testing.T) {
   394→	tempDir := t.TempDir()

   396→
   397→	// Create settings with user deny rule and entire deny rule

   410→
   411→	agent := &ClaudeCodeAgent{}

   422→	}

   425→	if containsDenyRule(perms.Deny, metadataDenyRuleTest) {

   427→	}
   428→}

   430→// readClaudeSettings reads and parses the Claude Code settings file
   431→func readClaudeSettings(t *testing.T, tempDir string) ClaudeSettings {
   432→	t.Helper()
   433→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(tempDir, ".claude", "settings.json")
   434→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath)

All tests pass. Here's a summary of the changes:

## Summary

Changed `.claude/settings.json` hooks handling to preserve unknown hook types.

**Files modified:**

1. **`cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/hooks.go`**
   - `InstallHooks`: Now uses `map[string]json.RawMessage` for hooks instead of typed `ClaudeHooks` struct
   - `UninstallHooks`: Same change to preserve unknown hook types
   - Added `parseHookType()` helper to parse specific hook types from the raw map
   - Added `marshalHookType()` helper to marshal hook types back to the raw map

2. **`cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/hooks_test.go`**
   - Added `TestInstallHooks_PreservesUnknownHookTypes` - verifies `Notification` and `SubagentStop` hooks are preserved when installing our hooks
   - Added `TestUninstallHooks_PreservesUnknownHookTypes` - verifies unknown hooks remain after uninstalling our hooks
   - Added `readRawHooks()` helper
   - Simplified `containsDenyRule()` using `slices.Contains`

**Behavior:**
- Unknown hook types like `Notification`, `SubagentStop`, `Setup` etc. are now preserved when Entire modifies the settings file
- Only the hook types we explicitly handle (SessionStart, SessionEnd, Stop, UserPromptSubmit, PreToolUse, PostToolUse) are parsed and modified
- All other hook types pass through unchanged

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/soph/Work/entire/devenv/cli/cmd/entire/cli/agent/claudecode/hooks_test.go",
  "old_string": "func TestInstallHooks_PreservesUnknownHookTypes(t *testing.T) {",
  "new_string": "func TestInstallHooks_PreservesUserHooksOnSameType(t *testing.T) {\n\ttempDir := t.TempDir()\n\tt.Chdir(tempDir)\n\n\t// Create settings with user hooks on the same hook types we use\n\twriteSettingsFile(t, tempDir, `{\n  \"hooks\": {\n    \"Stop\": [\n      {\n        \"matcher\": \"\",\n        \"hooks\": [{\"type\": \"command\", \"command\": \"echo user stop hook\"}]\n      }\n    ],\n    \"SessionStart\": [\n      {\n        \"matcher\": \"\",\n        \"hooks\": [{\"type\": \"command\", \"command\": \"echo user session start\"}]\n      }\n    ],\n    \"PostToolUse\": [\n      {\n        \"matcher\": \"Write\",\n        \"hooks\": [{\"type\": \"command\", \"command\": \"echo user wrote file\"}]\n      }\n    ]\n  }\n}`)\n\n\tagent := &ClaudeCodeAgent{}\n\t_, err := agent.InstallHooks(false, false)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"InstallHooks() error = %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\trawHooks := readRawHooks(t, tempDir)\n\n\t// Verify Stop has both user hook and our hook\n\tvar stopMatchers []ClaudeHookMatcher\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(rawHooks[\"Stop\"], &stopMatchers); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to parse Stop hooks: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Count hooks - should have user hook + our hook\n\tstopHookCount := 0\n\thasUserStopHook := false\n\thasEntireStopHook := false\n\tfor _, m := range stopMatchers {\n\t\tfor _, h := range m.Hooks {\n\t\t\tstopHookCount++\n\t\t\tif h.Command == \"echo user stop hook\" {\n\t\t\t\thasUserStopHook = true\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif h.Command == \"entire hooks claude-code stop\" {\n\t\t\t\thasEntireStopHook = true\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif !hasUserStopHook {\n\t\tt.Error(\"user Stop hook was not preserved\")\n\t}\n\tif !hasEntireStopHook {\n\t\tt.Error(\"Entire Stop hook was not added\")\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify SessionStart has both hooks\n\tvar sessionStartMatchers []ClaudeHookMatcher\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(rawHooks[\"SessionStart\"], &sessionStartMatchers); err != nil {\n\t\tt.Fatalf(\"failed to parse SessionStart hooks: %v\", err)\n\t}\n\thasUserSessionHook := false\n\thasEntireSessionHook := false\n\tfor _, m := range sessionStartMatchers {\n\t\tfor _, h := range m.Hooks {\n\t\t\tif h.Command == \"echo user session start\" {\n\t\t\t\thasUserSessionHook = true\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif h.Command == \"entire hooks claude-code session-start\" {\n\t\t\t\thasEntireSessionHook = true\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif !hasUserSessionHook {\n\t\tt.Error(\"user SessionStart hook was not preserved\")\n\t}\n\tif !hasEntireSessionHook {\n\t\tt.Error(\"Entire SessionStart hook was not added\")\n\t}\n\n\t// Verify PostToolUse has user's Write hook AND our Task/TodoWrite hooks\n\tvar postToolUseMatchers []ClaudeHookMatcher\n\tif err := json.Unmarshal(rawHooks[\"PostToolUse\"], &postToolUseMatchers); **Test coverage for hook preservation:**

| Scenario | Install | Uninstall |
|----------|---------|-----------|
| User hooks on same type (e.g., user's `Stop` hook) | `TestInstallHooks_PreservesUserHooksOnSameType` ✓ | `TestUninstallHooks_PreservesUserHooks` ✓ (existed) |
| Unknown hook types (e.g., `Notification`) | `TestInstallHooks_PreservesUnknownHookTypes` ✓ | `TestUninstallHooks_PreservesUnknownHookTypes` ✓ |

The new test `TestInstallHooks_PreservesUserHooksOnSameType` verifies that when users have existing hooks on `Stop`, `SessionStart`, and `PostToolUse`, our install adds our hooks alongside theirs rather than replacing them.

   507→// assertHookExists checks that a hook with the given matcher and command exists
   508→func assertHookExists(t *testing.T, matchers []ClaudeHookMatcher, matcher, command, description string) {
   509→	t.Helper()
   510→	for _, m := range matchers {

   515→				}
   516→			}
   517→		}
   518→	}
   519→	t.Errorf("%s was not found (matcher=%q, command=%q)", description, matcher, command)
   520→}
