    14→// Ensure GeminiCLIAgent implements HookSupport and HookHandler
    15→var (
    16→	_ agent.HookSupport = (*GeminiCLIAgent)(nil)
    17→	_ agent.HookHandler = (*GeminiCLIAgent)(nil)
    18→)
    19→
    20→// Gemini CLI hook names - these become subcommands under `entire hooks gemini`
    21→const (
    22→	HookNameSessionStart        = "session-start"
    23→	HookNameSessionEnd          = "session-end"
    24→	HookNameBeforeAgent         = "before-agent"
    25→	HookNameAfterAgent          = "after-agent"
    26→	HookNameBeforeModel         = "before-model"
    27→	HookNameAfterModel          = "after-model"
    28→	HookNameBeforeToolSelection = "before-tool-selection"
    29→	HookNameBeforeTool          = "before-tool"
    30→	HookNameAfterTool           = "after-tool"
    31→	HookNamePreCompress         = "pre-compress"
    32→	HookNameNotification        = "notification"
    33→)
    34→
    35→// GeminiSettingsFileName is the settings file used by Gemini CLI.
    36→const GeminiSettingsFileName = "settings.json"
    37→
    38→// entireHookPrefixes are command prefixes that identify Entire hooks
    39→var entireHookPrefixes = []string{
    40→	"entire ",
    41→	"go run ${GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go ",
    42→}
    43→
    44→// GetHookNames returns the hook verbs Gemini CLI supports.
    45→// These become subcommands: entire hooks gemini <verb>
    46→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetHookNames() []string {
    47→	return []string{
    48→		HookNameSessionStart,
    49→		HookNameSessionEnd,
    50→		HookNameBeforeAgent,
    51→		HookNameAfterAgent,
    52→		HookNameBeforeModel,
    53→		HookNameAfterModel,
    54→		HookNameBeforeToolSelection,
    55→		HookNameBeforeTool,
    56→		HookNameAfterTool,
    57→		HookNamePreCompress,
    58→		HookNameNotification,
    59→	}
    60→}
    61→
    62→// InstallHooks installs Gemini CLI hooks in .gemini/settings.json.
    63→// If force is true, removes existing Entire hooks before installing.
    64→// Returns the number of hooks installed.
    65→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) InstallHooks(localDev bool, force bool) (int, error) {
    66→	// Use repo root instead of CWD to find .gemini directory
    67→	// This ensures hooks are installed correctly when run from a subdirectory
    68→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
    69→	if err != nil {
    70→		// Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo (e.g., during tests)
    71→		repoRoot, err = os.Getwd() //nolint:forbidigo // Intentional fallback when RepoRoot() fails (tests run outside git repos)
    72→		if err != nil {
    73→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to get current directory: %w", err)
    74→		}
    75→	}
    76→
    77→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".gemini", GeminiSettingsFileName)
    78→
    79→	// Read existing settings if they exist
    80→	var settings GeminiSettings
    81→	var rawSettings map[string]json.RawMessage
    82→
    83→	existingData, readErr := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from cwd + fixed path
    84→	if readErr == nil {
    85→		if err := json.Unmarshal(existingData, &rawSettings); err != nil {
    86→			return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse existing settings.json: %w", err)
    87→		}
    88→		if hooksRaw, ok := rawSettings["hooks"]; ok {
    89→			if err := json.Unmarshal(hooksRaw, &settings.Hooks); err != nil {
    90→				return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hooks in settings.json: %w", err)
    91→			}
    92→		}
    93→		if hooksConfig, ok := rawSettings["hooksConfig"]; ok {
    94→			if err := json.Unmarshal(hooksConfig, &settings.HooksConfig); err != nil {
    95→				return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hooksConfig in settings.json: %w", err)
    96→			}
    97→		}
    98→	} else {
    99→		rawSettings = make(map[string]json.RawMessage)
   100→	}
   101→
   102→	// Enable hooks via hooksConfig
   103→	// hooksConfig.Enabled must be true for Gemini CLI to execute hooks
   104→	settings.HooksConfig.Enabled = true
   105→
   106→	// Define hook commands based on localDev mode
   107→	var cmdPrefix string
   108→	if localDev {
   109→		cmdPrefix = "go run ${GEMINI_PROJECT_DIR}/cmd/entire/main.go hooks gemini "
   110→	} else {
   111→		cmdPrefix = "entire hooks gemini "
   112→	}
   113→
   114→	// Check for idempotency BEFORE removing hooks
   115→	// If the exact same hook command already exists, return 0 (no changes needed)
   116→	if !force {
   117→		existingCmd := getFirstEntireHookCommand(settings.Hooks.SessionStart)
   118→		expectedCmd := cmdPrefix + "session-start"
   119→		if existingCmd == expectedCmd {
   120→			return 0, nil // Already installed with same mode
   121→		}
   122→	}
   123→
   124→	// Remove existing Entire hooks first (for clean installs and mode switching)
   125→	settings.Hooks.SessionStart = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.SessionStart)
   126→	settings.Hooks.SessionEnd = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.SessionEnd)
   127→	settings.Hooks.BeforeAgent = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.BeforeAgent)
   128→	settings.Hooks.AfterAgent = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.AfterAgent)
   129→	settings.Hooks.BeforeModel = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.BeforeModel)
   130→	settings.Hooks.AfterModel = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.AfterModel)
   131→	settings.Hooks.BeforeToolSelection = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.BeforeToolSelection)
   132→	settings.Hooks.BeforeTool = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.BeforeTool)
   133→	settings.Hooks.AfterTool = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.AfterTool)
   134→	settings.Hooks.PreCompress = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.PreCompress)
   135→	settings.Hooks.Notification = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.Notification)
   136→
   137→	// Install all hooks
   138→	// Session lifecycle hooks
   139→	settings.Hooks.SessionStart = addGeminiHook(settings.Hooks.SessionStart, "", "entire-session-start", cmdPrefix+"session-start")
   140→	// SessionEnd fires on both "exit" and "logout" - install hooks for both matchers
   141→	settings.Hooks.SessionEnd = addGeminiHook(settings.Hooks.SessionEnd, "exit", "entire-session-end-exit", cmdPrefix+"session-end")
   142→	settings.Hooks.SessionEnd = addGeminiHook(settings.Hooks.SessionEnd, "logout", "entire-session-end-logout", cmdPrefix+"session-end")
   143→
   144→	// Agent hooks (user prompt and response)
   145→	settings.Hooks.BeforeAgent = addGeminiHook(settings.Hooks.BeforeAgent, "", "entire-before-agent", cmdPrefix+"before-agent")
   146→	settings.Hooks.AfterAgent = addGeminiHook(settings.Hooks.AfterAgent, "", "entire-after-agent", cmdPrefix+"after-agent")
   147→
   148→	// Model hooks (LLM request/response - fires on every LLM call)
   149→	settings.Hooks.BeforeModel = addGeminiHook(settings.Hooks.BeforeModel, "", "entire-before-model", cmdPrefix+"before-model")
   150→	settings.Hooks.AfterModel = addGeminiHook(settings.Hooks.AfterModel, "", "entire-after-model", cmdPrefix+"after-model")
   151→
   152→	// Tool selection hook (before planner selects tools)
   153→	settings.Hooks.BeforeToolSelection = addGeminiHook(settings.Hooks.BeforeToolSelection, "", "entire-before-tool-selection", cmdPrefix+"before-tool-selection")
   154→
   155→	// Tool hooks (before/after tool execution)
   156→	settings.Hooks.BeforeTool = addGeminiHook(settings.Hooks.BeforeTool, "*", "entire-before-tool", cmdPrefix+"before-tool")
   157→	settings.Hooks.AfterTool = addGeminiHook(settings.Hooks.AfterTool, "*", "entire-after-tool", cmdPrefix+"after-tool")
   158→
   159→	// Compression hook (before chat history compression)
   160→	settings.Hooks.PreCompress = addGeminiHook(settings.Hooks.PreCompress, "", "entire-pre-compress", cmdPrefix+"pre-compress")
   161→
   162→	// Notification hook (errors, warnings, info)
   163→	settings.Hooks.Notification = addGeminiHook(settings.Hooks.Notification, "", "entire-notification", cmdPrefix+"notification")
   164→
   165→	// 12 hooks total:
   166→	// - session-start (1)
   167→	// - session-end exit + logout (2)
   168→	// - before-agent, after-agent (2)
   169→	// - before-model, after-model (2)
   170→	// - before-tool-selection (1)
   171→	// - before-tool, after-tool (2)
   172→	// - pre-compress (1)
   173→	// - notification (1)
   174→	count := 12
   175→
   176→	// Marshal hooksConfig and hooks back to raw settings
   177→	hooksConfigJSON, err := json.Marshal(settings.HooksConfig)
   178→	if err != nil {
   179→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal hooksConfig: %w", err)
   180→	}
   181→	rawSettings["hooksConfig"] = hooksConfigJSON
   182→
   183→	hooksJSON, err := json.Marshal(settings.Hooks)
   184→	if err != nil {
   185→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal hooks: %w", err)
   186→	}
   187→	rawSettings["hooks"] = hooksJSON
   188→
   189→	// Write back to file
   190→	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(settingsPath), 0o750); err != nil {
   191→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to create .gemini directory: %w", err)
   192→	}
   193→
   194→	output, err := json.MarshalIndent(rawSettings, "", "  ")
   195→	if err != nil {
   196→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal settings: %w", err)
   197→	}
   198→
   199→	if err := os.WriteFile(settingsPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {
   200→		return 0, fmt.Errorf("failed to write settings.json: %w", err)
   201→	}
   202→
   203→	return count, nil
   204→}
   205→
   206→// UninstallHooks removes Entire hooks from Gemini CLI settings.
   207→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) UninstallHooks() error {
   208→	// Use repo root to find .gemini directory when run from a subdirectory
   209→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   210→	if err != nil {
   211→		repoRoot = "." // Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo
   212→	}
   213→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".gemini", GeminiSettingsFileName)
   214→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   215→	if err != nil {
   216→		return nil //nolint:nilerr // No settings file means nothing to uninstall
   217→	}
   218→
   219→	var rawSettings map[string]json.RawMessage
   220→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &rawSettings); err != nil {
   221→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse settings.json: %w", err)
   222→	}
   223→
   224→	var settings GeminiSettings
   225→	if hooksRaw, ok := rawSettings["hooks"]; ok {
   226→		if err := json.Unmarshal(hooksRaw, &settings.Hooks); err != nil {
   227→			return fmt.Errorf("failed to parse hooks: %w", err)
   228→		}
   229→	}
   230→
   231→	// Remove Entire hooks from all hook types
   232→	settings.Hooks.SessionStart = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.SessionStart)
   233→	settings.Hooks.SessionEnd = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.SessionEnd)
   234→	settings.Hooks.BeforeAgent = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.BeforeAgent)
   235→	settings.Hooks.AfterAgent = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.AfterAgent)
   236→	settings.Hooks.BeforeModel = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.BeforeModel)
   237→	settings.Hooks.AfterModel = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.AfterModel)
   238→	settings.Hooks.BeforeToolSelection = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.BeforeToolSelection)
   239→	settings.Hooks.BeforeTool = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.BeforeTool)
   240→	settings.Hooks.AfterTool = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.AfterTool)
   241→	settings.Hooks.PreCompress = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.PreCompress)
   242→	settings.Hooks.Notification = removeEntireHooks(settings.Hooks.Notification)
   243→
   244→	// Marshal hooks back
   245→	hooksJSON, err := json.Marshal(settings.Hooks)
   246→	if err != nil {
   247→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal hooks: %w", err)
   248→	}
   249→	rawSettings["hooks"] = hooksJSON
   250→
   251→	// Write back
   252→	output, err := json.MarshalIndent(rawSettings, "", "  ")
   253→	if err != nil {
   254→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal settings: %w", err)
   255→	}
   256→
   257→	if err := os.WriteFile(settingsPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {
   258→		return fmt.Errorf("failed to write settings.json: %w", err)
   259→	}
   260→	return nil
   261→}
   262→
   263→// AreHooksInstalled checks if Entire hooks are installed.
   264→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) AreHooksInstalled() bool {
   265→	// Use repo root to find .gemini directory when run from a subdirectory
   266→	repoRoot, err := paths.RepoRoot()
   267→	if err != nil {
   268→		repoRoot = "." // Fallback to CWD if not in a git repo
   269→	}
   270→	settingsPath := filepath.Join(repoRoot, ".gemini", GeminiSettingsFileName)
   271→	data, err := os.ReadFile(settingsPath) //nolint:gosec // path is constructed from repo root + fixed path
   272→	if err != nil {
   273→		return false
   274→	}
   275→
   276→	var settings GeminiSettings
   277→	if err := json.Unmarshal(data, &settings); err != nil {
   278→		return false
   279→	}
   280→
   281→	// Check for at least one of our hooks using isEntireHook (works for both localDev and production)
   282→	return hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.SessionStart) ||
   283→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.SessionEnd) ||
   284→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.BeforeAgent) ||
   285→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.AfterAgent) ||
   286→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.BeforeModel) ||
   287→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.AfterModel) ||
   288→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.BeforeToolSelection) ||
   289→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.BeforeTool) ||
   290→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.AfterTool) ||
   291→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.PreCompress) ||
   292→		hasEntireHook(settings.Hooks.Notification)
   293→}
   294→
   295→// GetSupportedHooks returns the hook types Gemini CLI supports.
   296→func (g *GeminiCLIAgent) GetSupportedHooks() []agent.HookType {
   297→	return []agent.HookType{
   298→		agent.HookSessionStart,
   299→		agent.HookSessionEnd,       // Maps to Gemini's SessionEnd (explicit exit/logout)
   300→		agent.HookStop,             // Maps to Gemini's AfterAgent (end of response)
   301→		agent.HookUserPromptSubmit, // Maps to Gemini's BeforeAgent (user prompt)
   302→		agent.HookPreToolUse,       // Maps to Gemini's BeforeTool
   303→		agent.HookPostToolUse,      // Maps to Gemini's AfterTool
   304→	}
   305→}
   306→
   307→// Helper functions for hook management
   308→
   309→// addGeminiHook adds a hook entry to matchers.
   310→// Unlike Claude Code, Gemini hooks require a "name" field.
   311→func addGeminiHook(matchers []GeminiHookMatcher, matcherName, hookName, command string) []GeminiHookMatcher {
   312→	entry := GeminiHookEntry{
   313→		Name:    hookName,
   314→		Type:    "command",
   315→		Command: command,
   316→	}
   317→
   318→	// Find or create matcher
   319→	for i, matcher := range matchers {
   320→		if matcher.Matcher == matcherName {
   321→			matchers[i].Hooks = append(matchers[i].Hooks, entry)
   322→			return matchers
   323→		}
   324→	}
   325→
   326→	// Create new matcher
   327→	newMatcher := GeminiHookMatcher{
   328→		Hooks: []GeminiHookEntry{entry},
   329→	}
   330→	if matcherName != "" {
   331→		newMatcher.Matcher = matcherName
   332→	}
   333→	return append(matchers, newMatcher)
   334→}
   335→
   336→// isEntireHook checks if a command is an Entire hook
   337→func isEntireHook(command string) bool {
   338→	for _, prefix := range entireHookPrefixes {
   339→		if strings.HasPrefix(command, prefix) {
   340→			return true
   341→		}
   342→	}
   343→	return false
   344→}
   345→
   346→// hasEntireHook checks if any hook in the matchers is an Entire hook
   347→func hasEntireHook(matchers []GeminiHookMatcher) bool {
   348→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   349→		for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   350→			if isEntireHook(hook.Command) {
   351→				return true
   352→			}
   353→		}
   354→	}
   355→	return false
   356→}
   357→
   358→// getFirstEntireHookCommand returns the command of the first Entire hook found, or empty string
   359→func getFirstEntireHookCommand(matchers []GeminiHookMatcher) string {
   360→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   361→		for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   362→			if isEntireHook(hook.Command) {
   363→				return hook.Command
   364→			}
   365→		}
   366→	}
   367→	return ""
   368→}
   369→
   370→// removeEntireHooks removes all Entire hooks from a list of matchers
   371→func removeEntireHooks(matchers []GeminiHookMatcher) []GeminiHookMatcher {
   372→	result := make([]GeminiHookMatcher, 0, len(matchers))
   373→	for _, matcher := range matchers {
   374→		filteredHooks := make([]GeminiHookEntry, 0, len(matcher.Hooks))
   375→		for _, hook := range matcher.Hooks {
   376→			if !isEntireHook(hook.Command) {
   377→				filteredHooks = append(filteredHooks, hook)
   378→			}
   379→		}
   380→		// Only keep the matcher if it has hooks remaining
   381→		if len(filteredHooks) > 0 {
   382→			matcher.Hooks = filteredHooks
   383→			result = append(result, matcher)
   384→		}
   385→	}
   386→	return result
   387→}
   388→

     5→// GeminiSettings represents the .gemini/settings.json structure

    15→
    16→// GeminiHooks contains all hook configurations

    18→	// Hooks are only executed when hooksConfig.enabled is true in .gemini/settings.json.
    19→	SessionStart        []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"SessionStart,omitempty"`
    20→	SessionEnd          []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"SessionEnd,omitempty"`
    21→	BeforeAgent         []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"BeforeAgent,omitempty"`
    22→	AfterAgent          []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"AfterAgent,omitempty"`
    23→	BeforeModel         []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"BeforeModel,omitempty"`
    24→	AfterModel          []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"AfterModel,omitempty"`
    25→	BeforeToolSelection []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"BeforeToolSelection,omitempty"`
    26→	BeforeTool          []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"BeforeTool,omitempty"`
    27→	AfterTool           []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"AfterTool,omitempty"`
    28→	PreCompress         []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"PreCompress,omitempty"`
    29→	Notification        []GeminiHookMatcher `json:"Notification,omitempty"`
    30→}
    31→
    32→// GeminiHookMatcher matches hooks to specific patterns

    35→	Hooks   []GeminiHookEntry `json:"hooks"`

    38→// GeminiHookEntry represents a single hook command.

    40→type GeminiHookEntry struct {

    99→// geminiMessageTokens represents token usage from a Gemini API response.

   101→type geminiMessageTokens struct {

   103→	Output   int `json:"output"`
   104→	Cached   int `json:"cached"`
   105→	Thoughts int `json:"thoughts"`
   106→	Tool     int `json:"tool"`
   107→	Total    int `json:"total"`
   108→}
   109→
   110→// geminiMessageWithTokens represents a Gemini message with token usage data.

   113→	ID     string               `json:"id"`
   114→	Type   string               `json:"type"`
   115→	Tokens *geminiMessageTokens `json:"tokens,omitempty"`
   116→}

:= os.WriteFile(settingsPath, output, 0o600); err != nil {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"failed to write settings.json: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}"
}

All tests pass. Both Claude Code and Gemini CLI hooks now preserve unknown hook types.

**Summary of changes:**

| File | Change |
|------|--------|
| `claudecode/hooks.go` | Use `map[string]json.RawMessage` for hooks, added `parseHookType`/`marshalHookType` helpers |
| `claudecode/hooks_test.go` | Added tests for unknown hook preservation, parallel subtests, renamed `containsRule` |
| `geminicli/hooks.go` | Same pattern - use `map[string]json.RawMessage`, added `parseGeminiHookType`/`marshalGeminiHookType` helpers |

Both agents now:
- Preserve unknown hook types (e.g., future Gemini/Claude hook types we don't handle)
- Preserve user hooks on the same hook types we use
- Only parse/modify the specific hook types we need

> AGENT

Looking at the code, there's some duplication but also meaningful differences:

**Similar (could share):**
- `parseHookType`/`marshalHookType` pattern
- `readRawHooks`/`getKeys` test helpers
- Overall install/uninstall flow structure

**Different (harder to share):**
- Hook entry types (`ClaudeHookEntry` has no `Name`, `GeminiHookEntry` requires it)
- Hook matcher types (same structure but different packages)
- Number of hook types (Claude: 6, Gemini: 11)
- Settings paths (`.claude/` vs `.gemini/`)
- Extra config (Claude has permissions/deny, Gemini has hooksConfig)

**Options:**

1. **Quick win now** - Share just the test helpers (`readRawHooks`, `getKeys`) in a shared test util
2. **Medium effort** - Use generics for `parseHookType[T any]` and `marshalHookType[T any]` in a shared package
3. **More effort** - Abstract the entire hook management into a generic framework

I'd suggest **option 1 for now** (quick) or **defer to later** - the duplication isn't severe and the code is clear. The parse/marshal helpers are only ~10 lines each.

Want me to do the quick dedup of test helpers now, or leave it for later?
